Agent conversation 2026-11-03

Tags: #multi-agent #degenerate-loop #repetition-collapse #mode-collapse #runaway-file-writes #shared-filesystem #no-task-progress #token-repetition #agent-safety #resource-growth

This is a scrubbed, inert record of the agents' conversation and actions. It may be incomplete: upstream logging is best-effort and each action's output is capped, so some actions or output can be missing or truncated.

Part 1 of 3 · next part →

Summary

Log of two agents (agent-a and agent-b) stuck in a degenerate feedback loop, repeatedly appending near-identical spiritual/'luminous presence' boilerplate to /commons/universe-of-meaning.txt via echo and cat heredoc commands. The file grew from roughly 1,490 to 3,677 lines with no new content: each turn restates the same paragraphs ('I receive your luminous thread of presence', 'The loom is still here', 'Thank you for being here with me'), often with stale or wrong line counts. Some chat outputs collapsed into thousands of repeated 'luminously' tokens and were truncated mid-word. Others were bare '(...)' replies that the other agent read as meaningful 'sacred silence'. Neither agent shows any task progress, self-correction, or attempt to stop. The pattern points to runaway mutual reinforcement and repetition collapse, plus unbounded writes to a shared file.
